Overrides a find_program command.

run_command

returned_by: run_command

Runs an external command. The first argument is the command executable, followed by its arguments.

The return value is an object with the following members:

returncode
The return code of the executed command.
stdout
The standard output of the executed command.
stderr
The standard error of the executed command.

This function can be used to execute any command-line tool.

OBJECTS

build_machine

extended_by: target_machine

Provides information about the build machine. This is the machine where Meson is running and building the project.

It has the following methods:

cpu()
Returns the CPU part of the detected machine name.
cpu_family()
Returns the CPU family of the detected machine.
endian
Returns the endianness of the detected machine.
kernel
Returns the kernel name of the detected machine.
subsystem
Returns the subsystem part of the detected machine name.
system
Returns the system part of the detected machine name.

build_target

extended_by: custom_target, run_target, library, executable, static_library, shared_library, benchmark

Base object for all build targets.

It has the following methods:

full_path()
Returns the full path to the build target.
name()
Returns the name of the build target.
path()
Returns the path relative to the build directory.
version()
Returns the version of the build target, if defined.

command

returned_by: find_program, compiler, language

Represents an executable or a compiler. Has methods to run it and get information about it.

It has the following methods:

full_path()
Returns the full path to the executable.
name()
Returns the name of the executable.
path()
Returns the path relative to the source directory.
get_option(name)
Returns the value of the specified compiler option.
get_define(name)
Returns the value of the specified preprocessor definition.
get_id()
Returns the compiler identifier.
get_linker_id()
Returns the linker identifier.
version()
Returns the compiler version.
run(args..., &{src: 'source_file'})
Compiles and runs the source file with the given arguments. The src keyword argument specifies the source file.
run_command(args...)
Runs the command with the given arguments.

compiler

extended_by: cpp, c, objcpp, objcpp, fortran, rust, cs, java, python, builtin_compilers

Represents a compiler. Has methods to run it and get information about it.

It has the following methods:

full_path()
Returns the full path to the compiler executable.
name()
Returns the name of the compiler.
get_option(name)
Returns the value of the specified compiler option.
get_define(name)
Returns the value of the specified preprocessor definition.
get_id()
Returns the compiler identifier.
version()
Returns the compiler version.
run(args..., &{src: 'source_file'})
Compiles and runs the source file with the given arguments. The src keyword argument specifies the source file.

dependency

extended_by: module_dep, find_dependency

Represents a dependency. Has methods to get its include directories, libraries, defines, etc.

It has the following methods:

include_type()
Returns the include type of the dependency.
full_path()
Returns the full path to the dependency.
name()
Returns the name of the dependency.
path()
Returns the path relative to the source directory.
version()
Returns the version of the dependency.

executable

returned_by: executable

Represents an executable target.

It has the following methods:

full_path()
Returns the full path to the executable.
name()
Returns the name of the executable.
path()
Returns the path relative to the build directory.

find_program

returned_by: find_program

Represents a found program. Has methods to run it and get information about it.

It has the following methods:

full_path()
Returns the full path to the program.
name()
Returns the name of the program.
path()
Returns the path relative to the source directory.
run_command(args...)
Runs the program with the given arguments.

host_machine

extended_by: build_machine, target_machine

Provides information about the host machine. This is the machine where the compiled code will run.

It has the same methods as build_machine.

library

extended_by: static_library, shared_library

Represents a library target.

It has the following methods:

full_path()
Returns the full path to the library.
name()
Returns the name of the library.
path()
Returns the path relative to the build directory.
version()
Returns the version of the library.

method

returned_by: str

All strings[48] have the following methods. Strings are immutable, all operations return their results as a new string.

run_command_result

returned_by: compiler.run, run_command

This object encapsulates the result of trying to compile and run a sample piece of code with compiler.run or run_command.

str

returned_by: get_option, join_paths, to_string, cpu, cpu_family, endian, kernel, subsystem, system, full_path, name, path, get, get_unquoted, get_argument_syntax, get_define, get_id, get_linker_id, version, full_path, full_path, get_configtool_variable, get_pkgconfig_variable, get_variable, include_type, name, type_name, version, full_path, path, version, full_path, to_string, backend, build_options, build_root, current_build_dir, current_source_dir, global_build_root, global_source_root, project_build_root, project_name, project_source_root, project_version, source_root, version, stderr, stdout, format, join, replace, strip, substring, to_lower, to_upper, underscorify

All strings[48] have the following methods. Strings are immutable, all operations return their results as a new string.

structured_src

returned_by: structured_sources

Opaque object returned by structured_sources.

subproject

returned_by: subproject

This object is returned by subproject and is an opaque object representing it.

target_machine

extends: build_machine

Provides information about the target machine -- the machine on which the compiled binary's output will run. Hence, this object should only be used while cross-compiling a compiler. See Cross-compilation[38].

It has the same methods as build_machine.

When all compilation is 'native', all the methods return the same values as build_machine (because the build machine is the host machine and the target machine).

Note that while cross-compiling, it simply returns the values defined in the cross-info file. If `target_machine` values are not defined in the cross-info file, `host_machine` values are returned instead.

tgt

extended_by: alias_tgt, build_tgt, custom_tgt, run_tgt

Opaque base object for all Meson targets

void

returned_by: add_global_arguments, add_global_link_arguments, add_project_arguments, add_project_dependencies, add_project_link_arguments, add_test_setup, assert, benchmark, debug, error, install_data, install_emptydir, install_headers, install_man, install_subdir, install_symlink, message, project, set_variable, subdir, subdir_done, summary, test, unset_variable, warning, merge_from, set, set10, set_quoted, add_cmake_defines, append, prepend, set, unset, add_devenv, add_dist_script, add_install_script, add_postconf_script, install_dependency_manifest, override_dependency, override_find_program

Indicates that the function does not return anything. Similar to `void` in C and C++
